A grown-up-sized long-sleeved belted printed romper in lace, with balloon-leg shorts, covered edge to edge in a realistic printed photo-texture that makes the shirt look like the real garment, designed to look like the outfit of an augmented street doctor. The print covers every part of the garment edge to edge, from the neckline to the hem of the bottom. The top shows a patched coat with tools, and the shorts show trousers cut to short-leg length. An attached hood at the neck is printed as a mask. The back of the suit shows the matching back of the outfit, printed edge to edge, from the collar or hood to the ankles or feet. The outfit's cloth covers the whole piece in one continuous print, collar, shoulders, sleeves, body and leg openings all in the outfit's own colours and textures. The print is cut exactly to the garment, neck, shoulders, armholes, sleeves and hem are all covered in the outfit's own fabric. The romper is a single garment, top and shorts joined at the waist, in one continuous print. All textures, transparencies and sheens are printed patterns on solid cotton. Only the outfit's fabric, trims and fastenings are pictured, spread across the entire garment as a continuous pattern. It is a complete costume of augmented street doctor in one piece, shown full length from collar to ankles, with a patched coat with tools, trousers on the legs, a mask (as the hood), boots (as the feet), a medkit sewn or attached to the suit. The upper half carries the top and the leg tubes carry the lower garment, so the whole outfit reads at once. Novelty embellishments, padded 3D patch pockets and terry-loop textured stripes. It reads as a joyful, exaggerated novelty onesie, stuffed with texture and detail.

An attached hood at the neck is printed as a crescent crown. Turn it round and the back carries the rear of the outfit, with nothing left plain. The outfit's cloth covers the whole piece in one continuous print, collar, shoulders, sleeves, body and leg openings all in the outfit's own colours and textures. The outfit's collar meets the real collar band, its shoulders sit on the real shoulder seams and its sleeves fill the real sleeves, all in one piece of printed cloth. The romper is a single garment, top and shorts joined at the waist, in one continuous print. Everything is flat ink on opaque cotton, lace, tulle, fur, feathers, sequins, armour and metal appear as printed patterns. The print is the garment itself, flat and unfolded over the garment, in its own cloth and colours. The suit is the whole moon queen outfit, not just a top, with a silver robe with stars, a crescent crown (as the hood). It is shown in full, from the neckline down to the ankle cuffs. Novelty embellishments, corduroy ridged panels and quilted puffy panels on the chest. The embellishments are sewn on as real raised fabric, so the suit has depth, texture and sparkle from every angle.

The top shows a dark leather harness, and the shorts show leather trousers cut to short-leg length. An attached hood at the neck is printed as a hood. The back of the suit shows the matching back of the outfit, printed edge to edge, from the collar or hood to the ankles or feet. The print is cut to the garment's exact shape, so the neck, shoulders, sleeves and leg openings are all covered in the outfit's own fabric. The outfit's collar meets the real collar band, its shoulders sit on the real shoulder seams and its sleeves fill the real sleeves, all in one piece of printed cloth. It is a one-piece adult romper, a top and shorts joined at the waist as one garment, printed as the outfit. Everything is flat ink on opaque cotton, lace, tulle, fur, feathers, sequins, armour and metal appear as printed patterns. The print is the garment itself, flat and unfolded over the garment, in its own cloth and colours. It may also show a flat printed bare chest in __clothing_uniform_tee_skin__ with printed chest hair where the outfit is worn open, all as flat ink. It is a complete costume of shadow elf assassin in one piece, shown full length from collar to ankles, with a dark leather harness, leather trousers on the legs, a hood (as the hood), boots (as the feet), twin daggers sewn or attached to the suit. The upper half carries the top and the leg tubes carry the lower garment, so the whole outfit reads at once. Novelty embellishments, padded 3D patch pockets and fabric flames, stars or stripes sewn along the sleeves and legs. It is a novelty costume garment, made to be bright, playful, over-the-top and a pleasure to touch.

The top shows a worn leather jerkin with strapped vials, and the shorts show trousers cut to short-leg length. Turn it round and the back carries the rear of the outfit, with nothing left plain. The outfit's cloth covers the whole piece in one continuous print, collar, shoulders, sleeves, body and leg openings all in the outfit's own colours and textures. The print is cut exactly to the garment, neck, shoulders, armholes, sleeves and hem are all covered in the outfit's own fabric. The romper is a single garment, top and shorts joined at the waist, in one continuous print. The garment is solid opaque cotton and every texture and sheen is a printed picture. Only the outfit's fabric, trims and fastenings are pictured, spread across the entire garment as a continuous pattern. A full wandering monster slayer costume, head to toe in a single suit, with a worn leather jerkin with strapped vials, trousers on the legs, boots (as the feet), a steel sword sewn or attached to the suit. Nothing of the outfit is missing and the legs of the suit show the lower garment. Novelty embellishments, holographic appliqué badges and fluffy sherpa patches at the elbows and knees. The embellishments are sewn on as real raised fabric, so the suit has depth, texture and sparkle from every angle.

An attached hood at the neck is printed as a tall ridged headpiece. Front and back are both fully printed, the back showing how the outfit looks from behind. Every part of the garment, from the collar band to the leg openings, is printed in the outfit's own cloth. The print is cut exactly to the garment, neck, shoulders, armholes, sleeves and hem are all covered in the outfit's own fabric. A printed adult playsuit romper, the outfit's upper garment on top and its lower garment cut to short length below, all one piece of cloth. The garment is solid opaque cotton and every texture and sheen is a printed picture. The print is the garment itself, flat and unfolded over the garment, in its own cloth and colours. It is a complete costume of alien diplomat in one piece, shown full length from collar to ankles, with a flowing iridescent robe, a tall ridged headpiece (as the hood). The upper half carries the top and the leg tubes carry the lower garment, so the whole outfit reads at once. Novelty embellishments, chunky pom-pom trim along the hood edge and cuffs and fluffy sherpa patches at the elbows and knees. The embellishments are sewn on as real raised fabric, so the suit has depth, texture and sparkle from every angle.
An easy-fitting adult-sized long-sleeved henley-placket printed romper in seersucker, with balloon-leg shorts, covered edge to edge in a high-detail print that runs onto the sleeves and around the back, designed to look like the outfit of a harvest king. The print covers every part of the garment edge to edge, from the neckline to the hem of the bottom. The top shows a straw cape. The sleeve hems are cut at an angle, slanted, pointed or curved, to follow the outfit's own cuffs, and the print follows that edge exactly. An attached hood at the neck is printed as a crown of wheat. The back of the suit shows the matching back of the outfit, printed edge to edge, from the collar or hood to the ankles or feet. Every part of the garment, from the collar band to the leg openings, is printed in the outfit's own cloth. The print is cut exactly to the garment, neck, shoulders, armholes, sleeves and hem are all covered in the outfit's own fabric. It is a one-piece adult romper, a top and shorts joined at the waist as one garment, printed as the outfit. The garment is solid opaque cotton and every texture and sheen is a printed picture. Only the outfit's fabric, trims and fastenings are pictured, spread across the entire garment as a continuous pattern. A full harvest king costume, head to toe in a single suit, with a straw cape, a crown of wheat (as the hood), a sickle sewn or attached to the suit. Nothing of the outfit is missing and the legs of the suit show the lower garment. Novelty embellishments, lurex-thread embroidery and mismatched sleeves and legs in different textures. It reads as a joyful, exaggerated novelty onesie, stuffed with texture and detail.
